Given a (nonnegative, sum S). Let c=conv(a,a), M=max(c). Solve:
Maximize sum(g)
s.t. conv(a,g)[k] <= M * η? maybe M (or M*threshold)
g >=0.

Maybe use "nearly flat" weights from a quadratic phase? Let a_i = positive part of 1 + ε cos(λ i + φ) + ε2 cos(μ i + φ2) ... This can create anti-correlations for many lags. We can optimize few Fourier modes. Let's analyze using Fourier. For a_i = 1 + sum_m r_m cos(θ_m i + φ_m), normalized mean 1 (zero sum cos). Self-convolution coefficients for interior lags k: approximately n [1 + sum pairs r_p r_q/2 cos(θ_p θ?)]. More precisely, product of two trigonometric sums over contiguous block of length m≈n gives terms with cos at lag k. By choosing modes, we can make the oscillatory part negative at lags where it would peak, and perhaps flatten. Nonnegativity requires |sum r cos| ≤1. With few low modes, can shape.

For a single cosine: a=1 + A cos(θ i+φ). Conv coefficient (full circular) =1 + (A^2/2) cos(θ k + 2φ?)? The amplitude is A^2/2. To flatten, we want add negative A^2/2 where base m maybe? But base in linear conv is m/n (triangular) not 1. For interior near center m≈n, base 1. A single cosine creates periodic peak up to 1+A^2/2 (worsens) and minima 1-A^2/2. Need multiple modes to keep all lags low. This is like minimax approximation of the triangular envelope by trigonometric polynomial with nonnegative time signal.

Fast convolution via FFT:
```
def convolve_lin(a):
 n=len(a); L=np.fft.next_fast_len(2*n-1)
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
 c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
 c=np.maximum(c,0) # remove negative roundoff
 return c
```
But np.fft.next_fast_len available? numpy.fft.next_fast_len yes. Or implement simple power-of-two: while L<2n-1: L*=2. For n up to 8192, L=16384 fine. For speed, use power-of-two.
Score fast: `max(c)/(sum(a)^2)`; return 2n*... If invalid inf.

Gradient:
```
def softmax_gradient(c, a, T):
 n=len(a)
 z=c/T
 z-=z.max()
 p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
 L=np.fft.next_fast_len(3*n-2) # pad p length 2n-1, a n
 P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); Pa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
 conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Pa,L)[:2*n-1]? Wait conv p,a length 3n-2. We need slice [n-1:2n-1]
 length conv = 3n-2. Slicing from n-1 to 2n-1 (n values). So L>=3n-2.
 return 2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check formula earlier conv(p,a)[n-1:2n-1]. Yes.

Let's design low-dimensional parametric candidates more concretely:

Function make_sequence_n(n, shape_code, params): returns a normalized sum=1, max maybe 1.
Shapes:
0 uniform: ones
1 ramp_mid: i+0.5
2 sine_zero_endpoint: 1 - cos(pi*(i+0.5)/n)
3 sine_half: 1 + rho * sin(2*pi*(i+0.5)/n) with rho grid [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]
4 cosine_half: 1 + rho * cos(2*pi*(i+0.5)/n)
5 quadratic ramp: a_i = ( (i+0.5)/n )^p, p grid [0.3,0.5,0.7,1,1.3,1.7,2]
6 anti sinusoid: z = sin(2π m (i+α)/n) but anti constraint? We'll generate.
7 random anti-smooth: for each trial, m modes odd? Let's just random.

But target likely requires optimized random local, not fixed.

Implement random sequence generator:
```
def random_sequence(n, rng, style):
 if style==0: # smooth lowpass
   K=min(n//2, 32)
   amps = np.exp(-np.arange(K)/ (n/10?)) * rng.normal(size=K)
   phases = rng.uniform(0,2π,K)
   z = sum amps[m]*cos(2π m i/n + phases[m])
   # symmetrize? maybe anti
   z -= z.mean()
   z /= (np.max(np.abs(z))+eps)
   a = 1 + rho*z; a=np.maximum(a,0); normalize
 style anti: generate z first half random, mirror negative, then lowpass.
```

Local softmax implementation robust:
```
def softmax_local(a, time_limit, rng=None, seed=0):
 n=len(a); a=a.copy(); S=a.sum(); a/=S
 best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
 T = 0.05 * max(fast_conv(a).max(),1e-300)
 base_lr=1.0
 it=0
 while time.monotonic()<time_limit:
   c = fast_conv(a)
   maxc=c.max()
   if maxc <=0: break
   if T > 1e-12*maxc: T *=0.98; if T<... 
   p = softmax(c/T)
   grad = grad_from_p(p,a)
   # optional random noise to escape
   if rng and it%10==0: grad += 0.01*max(abs(grad))*rng.normal...
   for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
      step = base_lr*factor
      a_new = a - step*grad
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
      s=a_new.sum()
      if s<=0: continue
      a_new/=s
      sc=fast_score(a_new)
      if sc<best_score-1e-12:
         best=a_new.copy(); best_score=sc; a=a_new; break
   # reduce base_lr if no improve? maybe if many fails
   fails+=1; if fails>5: base_lr*=0.5; fails=0
   T*=0.95
 return best,best_score
```

Anti-palindromic random:
```
def make_anti_random(n, rng, smooth=0):
 z=np.zeros(n)
 half=n//2
 z[:half]=rng.normal(size=half)
 z[n-half:] = -z[:half][::-1]
 if n%2: z[n//2]=0
 if smooth>1: z=np.convolve(z, np.ones(smooth)/smooth, 'same')
 z-=z.mean()
 if maxabs>0: z/=maxabs
 return mean+rho z
```
Nonnegative if rho≤1. Use rho grid.

Smooth random lowpass:
```
def make_smooth_random(n,rng,K=16,rho=0.8):
 k=np.arange(K)
 amps=rng.normal(size=K)*np.exp(-k/5)
 phases=rng.uniform(0,2π,K)
 i=np.arange(n)
 z=np.zeros(n)
 for m in range(1,K+1): z+=amps[m-1]*np.cos(2π*m*i/n+phases[m-1])
 # anti? z = 0.5(z - z[::-1]) to enforce first-order anti? This makes anti-palindromic: z_rev =? Let w=0.5(z - z[::-1]); then w[n-1-i]=0.5(z[n-1-i]-z[i])=-w[i]. Good.
 z = 0.5*(z - z[::-1])
 z-=mean; scale; rho
```
This manipulates non-symmetric smooth. Use various.

n=len(a); L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
c[c<0]=0.0
return c
```

```
s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 return inf
c=fast_conv(a)
return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s))
```

`grad_from_p(a,p)`:
```
n=len(a); L=1; while L<3*n-2: L*=2
P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
conv=np.fft.irfft(P*A,L)
return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need p length 2n-1. `np.fft.rfft(p,L)` pads zeros. Good.

```

LP build:
```
def build_lp_matrix(a, rows):
 n=len(a); rows=list(rows)
 row_idx=[]; col_idx=[]; vals=[]
 for r,k in enumerate(rows):
   start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(k,n-1)
   for j in range(start,end+1):
      val=a[k-j]
      if val>0:
         row_idx.append(r); col_idx.append(j); vals.append(float(val))
 return csr_matrix((vals,(row_idx,col_idx)), shape=(len(rows),n))
```

Detailed `_local_search`:
```
def _local_search(a, time_limit, rng, allow_lp=True):
    n=len(a)
    a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_fast_score(a)
    c=_fast_conv(a); maxc=c.max()
    T=0.1*max(1e-30, c.max()-min(c, initial=False?) )
    if not finite: T=1e-6
    base_lr=1.0
    no_improve=0
    last_lp=time.monotonic()-1000.0
    step_no=0
    while time.monotonic() + 5 < time_limit:
        step_no +=1
        # softmax gradient
        c=_fast_conv(a); p=_softmax_weights(c,T)
        g=_grad_from_p(p,a)
        # optional zero out tiny?
        improved=False
        # line search factors
        for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
            step=base_lr*factor
            cand=a - step*g
            cand=_normalize_nonneg(cand)
            if cand is None: continue
            sc=_fast_score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                a=cand; best_sc=sc; best=a.copy(); T*=0.995; improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            no_improve +=1
            T = max(T*0.9, 1e-8*(c.max()+1e-300))
            base_lr = max(0.05, base_lr*0.9)
            if no_improve % 10 ==0:
               # random perturb
               for sigma in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
                  cand=a.copy(); w=...; cand[start:start+w]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,size=w))
                  cand=_normalize_nonneg(cand)
                  sc=_fast_score(cand)
                  if sc<best_sc-1e-12: ...; break
        else: no_improve=0
        # occasional LP
        if allow_lp and n<=700 and time.monotonic()-last_lp > 30:
            last_lp=time.monotonic()
            lp_cand, lp_sc=_lp_improve(a)
            if lp_cand is not None and lp_sc < best_sc-1e-12:
                a=lp_cand; best=...; T=0.1*...
    return best,best_sc
```

Moving average function helper:
```
def _moving_average(x, width):
 if width<=1: return x
 k=np.ones(width)/width
 return np.convolve(x,k,mode='same')[:len(x)]
```
`np.convolve` direct length n*width; width small ok.

     ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step functions minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2

The program uses:
- deterministic and randomized candidate generation, including anti-palindromic smooth
  low-discrepancy sign patterns and Thue-Morse patterns;
- fast FFT-based convolution scoring for local search;
- projected soft-max gradient steps on the convolution peak;
- random multiplicative perturbations to escape local minima;
- occasional exact LP-based direction search for moderate sizes;
- final exact evaluation with a safe fallback.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LP = False

_SAFE_SMALL = np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 0.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
_FINAL_MAX = 100.0


def _candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _next_pow2(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _fast_conv(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _fast_score(a):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def _softmax_weights(c, T):
    if T <= 1e-300:
        m = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[m] = 1.0
        return w
    z = c / T
    z -= float(z.max())
    p = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 1e-300:
        m = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[m] = 1.0
        return w
    return p / ps


def _grad_from_p(p, a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(P * A, L)
    if conv.size < 2 * n - 1:
        pad = np.zeros(2 * n - 1 - conv.size, dtype=np.float64)
        conv = np.concatenate((conv, pad))
    g = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g


def _scale_for_eval(a, maxval=_FINAL_MAX):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    m = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size if a.size else 8, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / m * float(maxval)
    # keep strictly below the evaluator's 1000 clip if maxval is close to it
    if a.size and a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / (float(a.max()) + 1e-12)) * 0.999999
    return a


def _exact_score(a):
    seq = _scale_for_eval(a)
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _moving_average(x, width):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    width = int(max(1, min(width, n)))
    if width <= 1:
        return x.copy()
    k = np.ones(width, dtype=np.float64) / float(width)
    y = np.convolve(x, k, mode="same")
    if y.size != n:
        y = y[:n]
    return y


def _base_anti_z(n, rng, style=0, smooth=3, K=None):
    n = int(n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 2:
        return z
    if K is None:
        K = min(max(4, n // 2), 32)
    K = int(max(1, min(K, n)))

    if style == 0:
        half = n // 2
        z[:half] = rng.normal(size=half)
        z[n - half:] = -z[:half][::-1]
        if n % 2:
            z[n // 2] = 0.0
    elif style == 1:
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        denom = float(n)
        for m in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = rng.normal() * np.exp(-float(m) / 6.0)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / denom + ph)
        z = 0.5 * (z - z[::-1])
    else:
        half = n // 2
        z0 = np.zeros(half, dtype=np.float64)
        z0 = rng.normal(size=half)
        z0 = _moving_average(z0, max(1, smooth))
        z0 = np.sign(z0)
        z[:half] = z0
        z[n - half:] = -z0[::-1]
        if n % 2:
            z[n // 2] = 0.0
        if smooth > 1:
            z = _moving_average(z, smooth)
            z = 0.5 * (z - z[::-1])

    z -= float(z.mean())
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(z))) if z.size else 0.0
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return z / m


def _thue_morse_z(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    parity = [0] * n
    for i in range(1, n):
        parity[i] = parity[i >> 1] ^ (i & 1)
    half = n // 2
    first = np.zeros(half, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(half):
        first[i] = -1.0 if (parity[i] & 1) else 1.0
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:half] = first
    z[n - half:] = -first[::-1]
    if n % 2:
        z[n // 2] = 0.0
    z -= float(z.mean())
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
    if m > 1e-300:
        z /= m
    return z


def _dirichlet_candidate(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    # Mix in a small uniform floor to avoid too-sparse invalid-looking locals.
    a = 0.65 * a + 0.35 * (1.0 / float(n))
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)


def _add_candidate(out, a):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return
    sc = _fast_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        out.append((sc, a.copy()))


def _lp_improve(a):
    if not _HAVE_LP:
        return None, float("inf")
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2 or n > 600:
        return None, float("inf")

    c = _fast_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None, float("inf")

    if 2 * n - 1 <= 1200:
        rows = list(range(2 * n - 1))
    else:
        s = int(np.argmax(a))
        rows = [s + j for j in range(n)]
        idx = np.argsort(c)
        for k in idx:
            k = int(k)
            if k not in rows:
                rows.append(k)
                if len(rows) >= 1200:
                    break
    rows = list(set(rows))

    A = np.zeros((len(rows), n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(rows):
        k = int(k)
        start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        end = min(k, n - 1)
        if end < start:
            continue
        A[r, start:end + 1] = a[k - start:k - end - 1:-1]

    b = np.full(len(rows), M, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if res.x is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    T = float(g.sum())
    if T <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
        return None, float("inf")

    g /= T
    sc0 = _fast_score(a)
    best_cand = None
    best_sc = sc0

    for tau in (0.03, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8):
        cand = (1.0 - tau) * a + tau * g
        cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = _fast_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand

    return best_cand, best_sc


def _local_search(a, time_limit, rng, allow_lp=True):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return _SAFE_SMALL, float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)

    c0 = _fast_conv(a)
    max0 = float(c0.max()) if c0.size else 1.0
    T = 0.1 * max0 if max0 > 1e-300 else 1e-6
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _fast_score(a)
    base_lr = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    last_lp = time.monotonic() - 1000.0

    while time.monotonic() + 2.0 < time_limit:
        c = _fast_conv(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if maxc <= 1e-300:
            break

        p = _softmax_weights(c, T)
        g = _grad_from_p(p, a)

        improved = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
            step = base_lr * factor
            cand = a - step * g
            cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                improved = True
                T = max(T * 0.995, 1e-14 * maxc)
                base_lr = 1.0
                no_improve = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            T = max(T * 0.9, 1e-9 * (maxc + 1e-300))
            base_lr = max(0.05, base_lr * 0.85)

            if no_improve % 5 == 0 or no_improve >= 20:
                # multiplicative block perturbations
                for sigma in (0.04, 0.08, 0.16, 0.32):
                    w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 100), max(2, n // 3)))
                    w = max(1, min(w, n))
                    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
                    stop = min(n, start + w)
                    cand = a.copy()
                    cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=stop - start))
                    cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    sc = _fast_score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        a = cand
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                        T = 0.1 * float(_fast_conv(a).max())
                        no_improve = 0
                        improved = True
                        break

                # global anti-balanced perturbation
                if not improved and no_improve >= 10:
                    style = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
                    sm = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 4))
                    K = max(4, min(n, 8 + int(rng.integers(0, 24))))
                    z = _base_anti_z(n, rng, style, sm, K)
                    for amp in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
                        cand = a * (1.0 + amp * z)
                        cand = _normalize_nonneg(cand)
                        if cand is None:
                            continue
                        sc = _fast_score(cand)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            a = cand
                            best = cand.copy()
                            best_sc = sc
                            T = 0.1 * float(_fast_conv(a).max())
                            no_improve = 0
                            improved = True
                            break

        # LP-based refinement, only for moderate n and not too frequently.
        if (allow_lp and _HAVE_LP and n <= 600 and
                time.monotonic() - last_lp > 30.0 and
                time.monotonic() + 90.0 < time_limit):
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            cand, sc = _lp_improve(a)
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                best = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                c2 = _fast_conv(a)
                T = 0.08 * float(c2.max()) if c2.size else 1e-6
                no_improve = 0

    return best, best_sc


def _upsample(a, n2):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    n2 = int(n2)
    if n2 <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return a
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(n2, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if n >= n2:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n2)
        return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n2)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """Return the best sequence found within the time budget."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = start + total - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    candidates = []

    _add_candidate(candidates, _SAFE_SMALL)

    # Include the externally provided best known sequence if available.
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        hs = None
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            a0 = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            a0 = a0[np.isfinite(a0)]
            if a0.size > 4:
                if a0.size > 8192:
                    a0 = _upsample(a0, 8192)
                _add_candidate(candidates, a0)
                if a0.size <= 8192:
                    z0 = _base_anti_z(int(a0.size), rng, 0, 2, max(4, min(16, a0.size // 2)))
                    _add_candidate(candidates, a0 * (1.0 + 0.12 * z0))
                    z1 = _base_anti_z(int(a0.size), rng, 1, 3, max(4, min(24, a0.size // 2)))
                    _add_candidate(candidates, a0 * (1.0 + 0.20 * z1))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Deterministic shapes for a selection of lengths.
    n_main = [4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 12, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]
    for n in n_main:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 900:
            break

        _add_candidate(candidates, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

        if n > 1:
            x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
            # powers / ramp-like profiles
            for p in (0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.8):
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(x ** p, 1e-9))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum((1.0 - x) ** p, 1e-9))

            # sine/cosine endpoint shapes
            for rho in (0.2, 0.45, 0.7, 0.9):
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 - np.cos(np.pi * x), 0.0) ** (1.0 / max(rho, 0.2)))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + rho * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 - rho * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + rho * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0))

            # Thue-Morse style
            zt = _thue_morse_z(n)
            for rho in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 0.95):
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + rho * zt, 0.0))

    # Randomized candidate generation.
    gen_deadline = min(start + 200.0, hard_deadline - 600.0)
    max_candidates = 900

    n_rand = [4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 12, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]
    counts = [30, 30, 25, 25, 20, 20, 18, 16, 16, 14, 12, 10, 8, 8, 6, 4]

    for idx, n in enumerate(n_rand):
        cnt = int(counts[idx]) if idx < len(counts) else 4
        for _ in range(cnt):
            if len(candidates) >= max_candidates or time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
                break

            if n <= 12:
                for _ in range(3):
                    _add_candidate(candidates, _dirichlet_candidate(n, rng))

            style = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            sm = 1 + int(rng.integers(0, 5))
            K = max(4, min(n, 8 + int(rng.integers(0, 24))))
            z = _base_anti_z(n, rng, style, sm, K)
            if float(np.max(np.abs(z))) > 1e-12:
                rho = float(0.25 + 0.75 * rng.random())
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + rho * z, 0.0))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + 0.6 * z, 0.0))
                _add_candidate(candidates, np.maximum(1.0 + 0.95 * z, 0.0))

    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_norm = None

    def update_best(norm_a):
        nonlocal best_exact, best_norm
        norm_a = _normalize_nonneg(norm_a)
        if norm_a is None:
            return
        sc = _exact_score(norm_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_norm = norm_a.copy()
            print("new best exact score = %.8f, n = %d" % (sc, norm_a.size), flush=True)

    update_best(_SAFE_SMALL)

    # Exact-evaluate all very small candidates immediately.
    for _, a_small in candidates:
        if a_small.size <= 64:
            update_best(a_small)

    # Select a handful of candidates for local search.
    selected = []
    seen_size_large = False
    for _, a in candidates:
        if len(selected) >= 6:
            break
        selected.append(a.copy())
        if a.size >= 1024:
            seen_size_large = True
    if not seen_size_large:
        for _, a in candidates:
            if a.size >= 1024:
                while len(selected) > 1:
                    selected.pop()
                selected.append(a.copy())
                break

    local_deadline = hard_deadline - 180.0
    print("local search candidates =", len(selected), "budget_left =",
          max(0.0, local_deadline - time.monotonic()), flush=True)

    for i, a_sel in enumerate(selected):
        remaining = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break

        if i == len(selected) - 1:
            alloc = max(0.0, remaining - 5.0)
        else:
            alloc = max(20.0, min(remaining * 0.32, 520.0))
        limit = time.monotonic() + alloc

        a_norm = _normalize_nonneg(a_sel)
        if a_norm is None:
            continue

        # Refine.
        a_opt, sc_fast = _local_search(a_norm, limit, rng, allow_lp=True)
        update_best(a_norm)
        update_best(a_opt)

        # If a very large length is in hand, try controlled upsampling a bit.
        if time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 120.0:
            target_len = max(int(a_opt.size) + max(256, a_opt.size // 2), 8192)
            target_len = min(target_len, 8192)
            if a_opt.size < target_len:
                a_up = _upsample(a_opt, target_len)
                a_up = _normalize_nonneg(a_up)
                if a_up is not None:
                    sc_up = _fast_score(a_up)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < sc_fast + 0.05:
                        # A little more local polish on the upscaled version.
                        local_limit = min(local_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
                        a_up2, _ = _local_search(a_up, local_limit, rng, allow_lp=True)
                        update_best(a_up2)
                        update_best(a_up)

    if best_norm is None:
        best_norm = _SAFE_SMALL.copy()

    # Final exact check and safe return.
    final_seq = [float(x) for x in _scale_for_eval(best_norm)]
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq))
    except Exception:
        final_score = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in _scale_for_eval(_SAFE_SMALL)]

    print("final returned score estimate = %.8f, n = %d" % (final_score, len(final_seq)), flush=True)
    return final_seq
```
